Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any autoimmunity module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 544. The projective resolution has length at most 500 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the autoimmunity space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 544 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 500. QED.
